/* JClones_DuValve — RNBO codebox~ port of JClones_DuValve (JSFX).
 * Source: repos/JSFXClones/jsfx/JClones_DuValve.jsfx
 * Copyright (c) JClones — MIT License (see repos/JSFXClones/NOTICE).
 * in1/in2: stereo in; out1/out2: stereo out.
 * A valve-stage emulation: input gain, a per-model low shelf, a low-pass into
 * the non-linear shaper, a 256-point measured transfer curve per dynamic model
 * (the negative half is a separate, measured half-curve), then a high-pass and
 * output gain. The spectral model selects the three one-pole corners and the
 * shelf amount around that shaper. Original slider units, ranges and defaults
 * kept.
 *
 * Table layout: s_dyn = 40 dynamic models x 512 points, base = model * 512,
 * negative half at base + 256. s_spGain / s_spLs / s_spHp / s_spLp = the 40
 * spectral models' low-shelf gain, low-shelf, high-pass and low-pass
 * coefficients (calibrated at 44.1 kHz, rescaled at run time).
 */
// Input (dB)
@param({ min: -12, max: 6 }) input_db = 0;
// Pusher (dB)
@param({ min: -6, max: 12 }) pusher_db = 0;
// Dynamic Model
@param({ min: 0, max: 39, steps: 39 }) dynamic_model = 0;
// Spectral Model
@param({ min: 0, max: 40, steps: 40 }) spectral_model = 0;
// Lobe Flip
@param({ enum: ["off", "on"] }) lobe_flip = 0;
// Output (dB)
@param({ min: -12, max: 6 }) output_db = 0;

@state s_dyn = new FixedFloat64Array(20480);
@state s_spGain = new FixedFloat64Array(40);
@state s_spLs = new FixedFloat64Array(40);
@state s_spHp = new FixedFloat64Array(40);
@state s_spLp = new FixedFloat64Array(40);
@state s_tabReady = 0;
@state s_dynBase = 0;
@state s_flip = 0;
@state s_lsG = 0;
@state s_lsK = 0;
@state s_hpK = 0;
@state s_lpK = 0;
@state s_inK = 1;
@state s_driveK = 1;
@state s_outK = 1;
@state s_st = new FixedFloat64Array(6);
@state frame: Int = 0;

function fnDbToK(x) {
    return pow(10.0, x / 20.0);
}

function fnSetGain(ig, dg, og) {
    s_inK = ig * 0.5;
    s_driveK = dg;
    s_outK = og * 2.0;
}

function fnSetModel(dyn, spec) {
    s_dynBase = dyn * 512.0;
    let si: Int = spec;
    let ss = 44100.0 / maximum(samplerate(), 1.0);
    s_lsG = s_spGain[si];
    s_lsK = s_spLs[si] * ss;
    s_hpK = s_spHp[si] * ss;
    s_lpK = s_spLp[si] * maximum(ss, 1.0 / 1.5);
}

function fnShape(x) {
    let base = s_dynBase;
    if (x <= 0.0) base = base + 256.0;
    let b: Int = base;
    let fe = abs(x) * 255.0;
    let y = 0.0;
    if (fe > 255.0) {
        y = s_dyn[b + 255];
    } else {
        let t0: Int = floor(fe);
        let t1: Int = t0 + 1;
        if (t1 > 255) t1 = 255;
        let k = fe - t0;
        y = s_dyn[b + t1] * k + s_dyn[b + t0] * (1.0 - k);
    }
    if (x <= 0.0) y = 0.0 - y;
    return y;
}

function fnProcess(x, c) {
    let k: Int = c * 3;
    let y = x * s_inK;

    // low shelf
    s_st[k] = s_st[k] + (y - s_st[k]) * s_lsK;
    y = y + s_st[k] * s_lsG;

    // low-pass into the shaper
    s_st[k + 1] = s_st[k + 1] + (y - s_st[k + 1]) * s_lpK;
    y = s_st[k + 1];

    if (s_flip >= 0.5) y = 0.0 - y;
    y = fnShape(y * s_driveK) / s_driveK;
    if (s_flip >= 0.5) y = 0.0 - y;

    // high-pass
    s_st[k + 2] = s_st[k + 2] + (y - s_st[k + 2]) * s_hpK;
    y = y - s_st[k + 2];

    return y * s_outK;
}
